**Bot autonomous decision (2026-08-06):** Widen the sector search beyond Tech (Rule 10 blocked) and Industrials (XLI still failing R:R), or stay patient another day on the two known paths? → **WIDENED the search — tested XLB (Materials), still no valid candidate; STAY PATIENT today.** → Materials (XLB) is the strongest confluence candidate available: it sits in the Leading momentum quadrant alongside XLI/XLE/XLP, carries a same-day Benzinga medium BUY signal, and Perplexity confirms the bullish case is macro-backed (Bloomberg Intelligence sees ~20% 2026 materials-sector earnings growth, ISM Manufacturing PMI 54.0, materials positioning near 1st-percentile underweight per Deutsche Bank). But the entry math fails the same way XLI has failed for 4 straight sessions: XLB trades ~$51.4-52 against a $54.14 52-week high, so a mandatory 10% stop (~$46.35, risk ~$5.15/share) against the nearest credible resistance (~$54.14, reward ~$2.64/share) produces R:R ≈0.51:1 — far below the 1.5:1 urgency floor. This is a structural pattern, not a one-off: low-volatility sector ETFs sitting near their highs can't clear a 1.5:1 R:R with a 10% stop without a genuine breakout catalyst first. **Lesson for future widening:** prioritize single-stock candidates with real breakout room over additional sector ETFs, which mechanically fail this same math. XLE (also Leading + Benzinga BUY) was not tested for R:R — passed over on concentration-risk grounds since XOM already provides full Energy exposure at ~19% of equity, the account's largest position. XLV/XLU/XLC carry Benzinga BUY signals but sit outside the Leading momentum quadrant (Weakening/Improving/Lagging respectively) and were deprioritized per the sector-momentum rule. Rule 10 Tech cooldown separately re-confirmed still active — SOXX has NOT stabilized (Jul 29 -5.9%, Jul 30 +8.5% partial rebound, Jul 31 flat — still a volatile bear-flag pattern, not two clean sessions of stabilization); AMD analysts are "doubling down" post-selloff but that's a fundamentals call, not sector-rotation confirmation.
